Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in relativity theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 374 and the invariant I(A,B) = 851 mod 1.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the relativity decomposition. By minimality, the element a^374 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 851-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)
